namespace FimmtopForManaged
{
	public class CompCxsPathHelper
	{
		private static marshal_context _marshalContext;

		public static string GetCommonCxsPath(IList<string> cxsPaths)
		{
			if (cxsPaths != null && cxsPaths.Count > 1)
			{
				int num = int.MaxValue;
				List<string[]> list = new List<string[]>();
				int num2 = 0;
				if (0 < cxsPaths.Count)
				{
					do
					{
						string text = cxsPaths[num2];
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
						{
							string[] array = text.Split('.');
							list.Add(array);
							int num3 = array.Length;
							num = ((num > num3) ? num3 : num);
						}
						num2++;
					}
					while (num2 < cxsPaths.Count);
				}
				string text2 = "";
				int num4 = 0;
				if (0 < num)
				{
					do
					{
						string text3 = list[0][num4];
						int num5 = 1;
						if (1 < list.Count)
						{
							do
							{
								string b = list[num5][num4];
								if (string.Equals(text3, b, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
								{
									num5++;
									continue;
								}
								return text2;
							}
							while (num5 < list.Count);
						}
						text2 = ((text2.Length > 0) ? $"{text2}.{text3}" : text3);
						num4++;
					}
					while (num4 < num);
				}
				return text2;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static string GetRelativeCxsPath(string cxsPath, string commonSegment)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cxsPath))
			{
				string text = commonSegment + ".";
				if (cxsPath.StartsWith(text, StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
				{
					return cxsPath.Substring(text.Length);
				}
			}
			return cxsPath;
		}

		public unsafe static char* ConvertToBSTR(string text)
		{
			if (_marshalContext == null)
			{
				_marshalContext = new marshal_context();
			}
			return _marshalContext.marshal_as<wchar_t *,System::String>(text);
		}

		public unsafe static string ConvertToString(char* text)
		{
			if (text != null)
			{
				return new string(text);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static string JoinPath(string first, string second)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(first))
			{
				return second;
			}
			if (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(second))
			{
				return first;
			}
			return $"{first}.{second}";
		}

		[return: MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.U1)]
		public unsafe static bool IsEmpty(char* text)
		{
			//IL_0011: Expected I, but got I8
			if (text != null)
			{
				char* ptr = text;
				if (System.Runtime.CompilerServices.Unsafe.ReadUnaligned<short>(text) != 0)
				{
					do
					{
						ptr = (char*)((ulong)(nint)ptr + 2uL);
					}
					while (System.Runtime.CompilerServices.Unsafe.ReadUnaligned<short>(ptr) != 0);
				}
				if ((long)((nint)((byte*)ptr - (nuint)text) >> 1) != 0)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}
	}

namespace FimmtopForManaged
{
	public interface IComplexCxsPathPreprocessor
	{
		string Preprocess(string cxsPath);
	}
	public class ManagedCxsPathParser
	{
		private static IComplexCxsPathPreprocessor _preprocessor;

		private static bool __preprocessorRetrieved;

		private static IUnityContainer _unityContainer;

		internal static string TryParseComplexCxsPath(string cxsPath)
		{
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(cxsPath))
			{
				IComplexCxsPathPreprocessor complexCxsPathPreprocessor = EnsurePreprocessor();
				if (complexCxsPathPreprocessor != null)
				{
					return complexCxsPathPreprocessor.Preprocess(cxsPath);
				}
			}
			return cxsPath;
		}

		internal unsafe static char* TryParseComplexCxsPath(char* cxsPath)
		{
			if (cxsPath != null)
			{
				IComplexCxsPathPreprocessor complexCxsPathPreprocessor = EnsurePreprocessor();
				if (complexCxsPathPreprocessor != null)
				{
					string text = new string(cxsPath);
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
					{
						return CompCxsPathHelper.ConvertToBSTR(complexCxsPathPreprocessor.Preprocess(text));
					}
				}
			}
			return cxsPath;
		}

		internal static void InitializeWithContainer(IUnityContainer unityContainer)
		{
			_unityContainer = unityContainer;
		}

		private static IComplexCxsPathPreprocessor EnsurePreprocessor()
		{
			if (!__preprocessorRetrieved && _unityContainer != null)
			{
				_preprocessor = _unityContainer.TryResolve<IComplexCxsPathPreprocessor>();
				__preprocessorRetrieved = true;
			}
			return _preprocessor;
		}
	}
}

namespace <CrtImplementationDetails>
{
	[Serializable]
	internal class Exception : System.Exception
	{
		protected Exception(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
			: base(info, context)
		{
		}

		public Exception(string message, System.Exception innerException)
			: base(message, innerException)
		{
		}

		public Exception(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	internal class ModuleLoadException : System.Exception
	{
		public const string Nested = "A nested exception occurred after the primary exception that caused the C++ module to fail to load.\n";

		protected ModuleLoadException(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
			: base(info, context)
		{
		}

		public ModuleLoadException(string message, System.Exception innerException)
			: base(message, innerException)
		{
		}

		public ModuleLoadException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	internal class ModuleLoadExceptionHandlerException : <CrtImplementationDetails>.ModuleLoadException
	{
		private const string formatString = "\n{0}: {1}\n--- Start of primary exception ---\n{2}\n--- End of primary exception ---\n\n--- Start of nested exception ---\n{3}\n--- End of nested exception ---\n";

		private System.Exception <backing_store>NestedException;

		public System.Exception NestedException
		{
			get
			{
				return <backing_store>NestedException;
			}
			set
			{
				<backing_store>NestedException = value;
			}
		}

		protected ModuleLoadExceptionHandlerException(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
			: base(info, context)
		{
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(System.Exception);
			string name = "NestedException";
			NestedException = (System.Exception)info.GetValue(name, typeFromHandle);
			GC.KeepAlive(this);
		}

		public ModuleLoadExceptionHandlerException(string message, System.Exception innerException, System.Exception nestedException)
			: base(message, innerException)
		{
			NestedException = nestedException;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{

	internal class ModuleUninitializer : Stack
	{
		private static object @lock;

		internal static <CrtImplementationDetails>.ModuleUninitializer _ModuleUninitializer;

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		internal void AddHandler(EventHandler handler)
		{
			bool lockTaken = false;
			RuntimeHelpers.PrepareConstrainedRegions();
			try
			{
				RuntimeHelpers.PrepareConstrainedRegions();
				Monitor.Enter(@lock, ref lockTaken);
				RuntimeHelpers.PrepareDelegate(handler);
				Push(handler);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (lockTaken)
				{
					Monitor.Exit(@lock);
				}
			}
		}

		[SecurityCritical]
		static ModuleUninitializer()
		{
			@lock = new object();
			_ModuleUninitializer = new <CrtImplementationDetails>.ModuleUninitializer();
		}

		[SecuritySafeCritical]
		private ModuleUninitializer()
		{
			EventHandler value = SingletonDomainUnload;
			AppDomain.CurrentDomain.DomainUnload += value;
			AppDomain.CurrentDomain.ProcessExit += value;
		}

		[PrePrepareMethod]
		[SecurityCritical]
		private void SingletonDomainUnload(object source, EventArgs arguments)
		{
			bool lockTaken = false;
			RuntimeHelpers.PrepareConstrainedRegions();
			try
			{
				RuntimeHelpers.PrepareConstrainedRegions();
				Monitor.Enter(@lock, ref lockTaken);
				IEnumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator();
				try
				{
					while (enumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						((EventHandler)enumerator.Current)(source, arguments);
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					IEnumerator enumerator2 = enumerator;
					if (enumerator is IDisposable disposable)
					{
						disposable.Dispose();
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				if (lockTaken)
				{
					Monitor.Exit(@lock);
				}
			}
		}
	}
